Abstract: Evaluating detailed image captions from Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires going beyond surface-level semantic similarity. Reference-based metrics (e.g., CIDEr and SPICE) and LLM-as-scorer protocols struggle to verify dense factual claims, while existing QA-based alternatives generally offer lower probe density, narrower domain coverage, or no explicit alignment between individual questions and segmented image regions. We introduce CapProbe, a full-scene dense QA benchmark that turns detailed caption evaluation into region-aligned factual checking. Each image is decomposed into coarse semantic regions covering both foreground and background elements; for every retained region, we generate multiple-choice questions spanning 10 semantic categories, forming a dense checklist of probed visual facts. Guided by a two-tier taxonomy of 37 L1 domains and 219 L2 sub-domains, CapProbe comprises 346 images, 1,868 regions, and 25,650 questions, averaging 74 QA pairs per image. A language judge answers from the caption alone; an Uncertain option and Effective Accuracy provide a judge-dependent proxy for distinguishing unanswered probes from incorrectly resolved ones, while density-based metrics penalize verbose yet uninformative captions. The protocol is cost-effective: by converting unconstrained scalar scoring into structured MCQ reading, it reduces open-ended scoring bias while remaining judge-conditioned and yields relatively stable model rankings under a fixed reader. Experiments on 13 VLMs show large Coverage gaps across models, a clear competency-efficiency trade-off, and failure modes that sparse or overlap-based evaluation often misses. The benchmark data, annotations, and evaluation code will be released soon.

</details>


### [104] [Static in Frames, Dynamic in Events: Rethinking Features in Event Cameras as Motion Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11075)
*Hesam Araghi,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tomen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析事件相机角点检测中使用的两个特征（结构张量特征值和时空密度值），证明它们是"运动线索"（motion cues），并验证将这些特征与局部几何信息结合能够增强运动估计任务（尤其是光流估计）的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机以异步方式捕捉强度变化，具有高时间分辨率，其数据本质编码了场景动态和物体运动信息，因此从事件中提取的特征可能表现出帧基视觉中没有直接对应物的行为。作者希望探究这些"运动线索"特征的理论基础，并将它们用于增强运动估计任务。

Method: 1) 理论上分析移动角点处结构张量特征值与运动方向的关系；2) 在合成数据集上设计受控实验，验证将局部几何特征与特征值和密度值扩展后能提供互补的运动信息，且对纹理和散粒噪声鲁棒；3) 将所提出的特征集成到最先进的基于事件的光流网络中，在真实DSEC基准上评估。

Result: 在DSEC基准上，添加特征持续提升了光流精度，在数据稀缺场景和低容量模型中提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 结构张量特征值和时空密度值确实是有效的运动线索，与局部几何信息结合可为光流估计提供互补信息，尤其在小数据场景和轻量模型中收益更大。

Abstract: Event cameras capture intensity changes asynchronously with high temporal resolution, requiring novel preprocessing methods for downstream tasks. Unlike static intensity snapshots, event data inherently encode information about scene dynamics and object motion, meaning that features derived from events can exhibit behaviors with no direct analogue in frame-based vision. In this paper, we analyze two features used in event-based corner detection---the eigenvalues of the structure tensor and the spatiotemporal density values---and show that they are \emph{motion cues}. We hypothesize that these features, combined with local geometric information, can enhance motion estimation tasks. To validate this, we first theoretically analyze how the eigenvalues of the structure tensor at moving corner points relate to the direction of motion. We then design controlled experiments on a synthetic dataset, confirming that extending local geometric features with eigenvalues and density values provides complementary motion information and is robust to texture and shot noise. Finally, we integrate the proposed features into a state-of-the-art event-based optical flow network and evaluate on the real-world DSEC benchmark, where the added features consistently improve accuracy, with the largest gains in data-scarce scenarios and for lower-capacity models. The code for this paper can be found at: \href{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}.

</details>


### [105] [Foundation Model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling (FEEDS): A label-efficient training strategy for pan-cancer, multi-tracer PET/CT datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11076)
*Biratal Raj Wagle,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Grant Chau,Matthew E. Maeder,Muhammad Azeem Arshad,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEEDS是一种基于视觉基础模型嵌入的高效数据采样策略，通过选择最具信息量和多样性的未标注病例进行专家标注，在减少70%标注负担的同时达到与100%全标注训练相当的病灶分割性能，并在多数据集、多示踪剂和多种癌症类型上展现良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT中的自动病灶分割对癌症检测、分期和治疗规划有重要临床价值，但训练高性能分割模型需要大量标注数据，而这些数据的创建既耗时又需要专业知识。现有模型因标注数据有限而缺乏临床所需的准确性和泛化能力，因此需要一种标签和计算高效的深度学习策略来解决标注稀缺问题。

Method: FEEDS利用视觉基础模型的嵌入表示，从大规模未标注临床数据中选择最具信息量和多样性的病例用于专家标注。与无监督、半监督和主动学习方法不同，FEEDS采用一步式训练范式，只需有限的代表性训练集即可完成训练。在AutoPET-III数据集上训练和验证，并在AutoPET-III、DeepPSMA和Dartmouth-Hitchcock医学中心三个独立测试集上评估。

Result: FEEDS在所有三个测试集以及FDG和PSMA示踪剂、多种疾病上均展现良好泛化能力，优于随机采样标注、伪标签半监督学习以及仅用有限标注数据训练的方法。FEEDS以70%更少的标注负担达到了与100%全标注训练相当的性能，并在体素、病灶和解剖区域三个层面验证了临床实用性。

Conclusion: FEEDS为自动病灶分割中标签稀缺问题提供了一种实用解决方案，通过从大规模未标注临床数据仓库中构建代表性和多样化的标注队列，实现了标签与计算效率的双重优化，有望促进临床部署。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT imaging can assist clinicians with cancer detection, staging, and treatment planning across radiotracers and cancer types. However, training lesion segmentation models that capture variations in lesion size, distribution, and appearance requires large annotated datasets, whose creation is both time- and expertise-intensive. As a result, models trained on limited labeled PET/CT data often lack the accuracy and generalizability needed for clinical use. We present FEEDS (Foundation model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling), a label- and compute-efficient learning strategy that uses vision foundation model embeddings to select the most informative and diverse unlabeled cases for expert annotation. Unlike unsupervised, semi-supervised, and active learning approaches, FEEDS is a one-step training paradigm requiring only a limited, representative training set, making it label- and compute-efficient. We train and validate FEEDS using the AutoPET-III dataset. We test its accuracy and generalizability on three held-out sets: AutoPET-III, DeepPSMA, and an internal Dartmouth-Hitchcock Medical Center dataset. We evaluate clinical utility at the voxel, lesion, and anatomic region level to assess performance in high-risk areas and treatment planning utility. FEEDS outperforms random-sampling-based labeling, pseudolabel-based semi-supervised learning, and training with limited labeled data alone. It generalizes across all three test sets, FDG and PSMA tracers, and multiple diseases, matching fully-labeled (100\%) training performance with 70\% less annotation burden. FEEDS addresses the challenge of label scarcity in an automatic lesion segmentation framework by providing a practical approach for constructing representative and diverse annotation queues from large, unannotated clinical repositories.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Gaussian Structure: Intervention-Guided Density Control for Feed-Forward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11077)
*Hang Li,Jiahe Li,Meiying Gu,Jin Zheng,Lina Yu,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LGS框架，通过Gaussian Densify Policy学习剪枝/添加决策来动态调整高斯结构，并利用Cross-Time Point Query显式聚合跨时间戳特征，提升前馈高斯重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的前馈高斯重建方法将初始化的原始点集直接视为最终表示，无法像优化式3DGS那样在训练中累积梯度来决定表示是否应该稠密化；且共享的稀疏骨干网络仅隐式融合不同时间戳的观测，未显式聚合跨时间证据。

Method: LGS包含两个核心组件：(1) Gaussian Densify Policy，基于"剪枝或添加干预引发的局部梯度响应变化可反映结构调整是否有益"的观察，学习Densify Map（包含剪枝分数与添加分数），在推理时直接调整高斯结构；(2) Cross-Time Point Query，显式检索并聚合其他时间戳的Gaussian primitive邻域特征，用于可靠的属性预测。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上的大量实验表明，LGS一致性地优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LGS通过可学习的结构稠密化策略与显式跨时间特征聚合，同时增强了高斯结构和primitive属性，为前馈高斯重建提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [107] [Every Packet Counts: Dispersing Information for Loss-Resilient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11096)
*Yuhang Wei,Chuqin Zhou,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的抗丢包图像压缩方案，通过通道间能量重分布（ICR）、交错通道分组（ICG）和双层双分支自回归结构，同时解决打包阶段的非均匀信息分布和熵编码阶段的顺序解码依赖问题，在20%丢包率下较现有方法提升1.84 dB PSNR并将方差降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型图像压缩（LIC）虽在率失真性能上表现优异，但在卫星和应急通信等场景中极易受到数据包丢失的影响。这种脆弱性源于打包阶段的非均匀信息分布（关键信息集中在少量通道）和熵编码阶段的顺序解码依赖（丢包导致级联错误传播），现有方法未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) 通道间重分布（ICR）机制，在打包前重新分配通道能量，防止关键信息集中在少数通道；(2) 交错通道分组（ICG）策略，以跨步方式划分潜在通道以将信息分散到各数据包中，并控制每个包的尺寸；(3) 两层双分支自回归结构，通过缩短依赖链来限制丢包引起的级联错误传播。

Result: 方法在重建质量和稳定性上均优于现有方法。在20%丢包率下，较LossResilientLIC平均PSNR提升1.84 dB，PSNR方差降低一个数量级。仅在均匀随机丢包下训练的模型，能泛化到Gilbert-Elliott信道建模的突发性丢包场景，并优于专门针对该条件训练的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过同时解决打包阶段的非均匀信息分布和熵编码阶段的顺序解码依赖问题，可以在保持压缩性能的同时显著增强鲁棒性；且该方法对突发性丢包具有良好的泛化能力，无需针对特定丢包模式进行训练即可在实际信道条件下表现出色。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved impressive rate-distortion performance. However, existing methods remain highly vulnerable to packet loss, a common challenge in satellite and emergency communications. This vulnerability stems from non-uniform information distribution at the packetization stage and sequential decoding dependencies at the entropy coding stage. We propose an end-to-end loss-resilient image compression scheme that addresses both. Before packetization, we introduce an Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR) mechanism to redistribute channel energy, preventing critical information concentrating in a small subset of channels. Then, an Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG) strategy partitions latent channels in a strided manner to disperse information across packets, with each packet kept within constrained sizes. To limit cascading errors from lost packets, we adopt a two-layer dual-branch autoregressive structure to shorten the dependency chain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in both reconstruction quality and stability. At 20% packet loss, it achieves an average PSNR gain of 1.84 dB over LossResilientLIC while reducing PSNR variance by an order of magnitude. Notably, trained under uniform random loss only, our model generalizes to bursty loss modeled by the Gilbert-Elliott channel, outperforming methods explicitly trained for such conditions.

</details>


### [108] [AlbumentationsX: One Augmentation Pipeline for Images and Related Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11123)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumentationsX是一个数据增强库，通过将变换列表、概率、注释设置和随机种子封装在单个Compose对象中，确保图像及其所有注释（掩码、边界框、关键点、立体视图、视频帧或体积）在每次调用时使用相同的随机参数，避免数据不对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强过程中，图像与注释（如掩码、边界框、关键点等）可能被赋予不同的随机变换（如裁剪坐标不同），导致训练样本被破坏且注释与图像错位。传统的独立代码路径可能导致这种静默的对齐错误。

Method: AlbumentationsX将所有变换列表、概率、注释设置和随机种子保持在一个Compose对象中。每次调用仅选择一次随机值，并将其统一应用于训练样本的所有支持部分。库将对象的掩码、框和标签保持在一起，允许项目添加自定义变换，并支持保存pipeline定义、可视化单次调用过程和重放该调用。

Result: 该方法通过示例展示了Compose放置在文件解码为数组之后、PyTorch分批之前的流程，能够可靠地执行声明的变换并保持注释对齐。

Conclusion: AlbumentationsX通过集中化的随机选择和统一的变换应用机制解决了数据增强中的注释错位问题，同时保留了对变换选择的灵活性和对pipeline的可复现性支持。

Abstract: Augmentation can corrupt a training example when an image and its annotations receive different random changes. A crop must use the same coordinates for the image, mask, boxes, keypoints, stereo views, video frames, or volume. Code paths that choose these values separately can silently misalign the data.
  AlbumentationsX keeps the transform list, probabilities, annotation settings, and random seed in one Compose object. Each call chooses random values once and applies them to every supported part of the training example. The library keeps each object's mask, box, and label together and lets projects add their own transforms. It can also save the pipeline definition, show what happened in one call, and run that call again.
  The examples place Compose after files have been decoded into arrays and before PyTorch groups examples into a batch. AlbumentationsX executes the declared transforms. Practitioners still decide whether a flip, crop, color change, or other operation preserves the correct label for their task.

</details>


### [109] [Is There Really a Camouflaged Object? Towards Realistic Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11135)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si,Wende Tan,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPC16K大规模基准数据集和OPCNet网络，将伪装物体检测从封闭世界假设拓展到开放世界场景，通过联合目标定位与伪装存在性推理，在实现精确分割的同时有效抑制纯背景和非伪装物体图像上的误检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数伪装物体检测(COD)方法基于封闭世界假设，即假定每张输入图像都包含伪装物体。这一假设忽略了现实场景中可能存在的纯背景图像或非伪装物体图像，导致模型在开放世界中部署时产生大量误检（假阳性）。因此需要构建包含负样本的真实场景基准并设计能够感知物体存在性的检测方法。

Method: 1) 构建OPC16K基准：包含16,245张来自14个来源的图像，分为伪装物体图像、纯背景图像和非伪装物体图像三类，支持分割质量和负样本拒绝能力的综合评估；2) 提出OPCNet存在感知伪装网络：将COD从纯分割任务重构为物体定位与伪装存在性推理的联合任务，包含三个关键模块——层级存在性推理（区分CO/BG/NOCOD三种场景）、相似性感知伪装关系建模（捕获前景-背景伪装线索）、存在性感知特征细化（用存在性预测调控分割特征）。

Result: 在OPC16K基准上的大量实验表明，OPCNet在提出的现实COD评估协议下取得了优越性能，在显著降低负样本误检的同时保持了准确的伪装物体分割精度。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入含负样本的开放世界基准OPC16K和存在感知网络OPCNet，弥补了现有COD方法在现实场景中过度假设的缺陷，为伪装物体检测从封闭世界走向开放世界提供了新的基准框架和解决方案。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that are visually concealed in their surroundings and has attracted increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing COD methods are developed under a closed-world assumption, where each input image is assumed to contain a camouflaged object. This assumption ignores realistic scenarios with pure backgrounds or non-camouflaged objects, causing existing models to produce severe false positives when deployed in open-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose OPC16K, a large-scale benchmark for realistic COD. OPC16K contains 16,245 images from 14 sources and is carefully organized into camouflaged-object images, pure background images, and non-camouflaged-object images, enabling comprehensive evaluation of both segmentation quality and negative-sample rejection. Based on this benchmark, we further propose OPCNet, a presence-aware camouflage network that reformulates COD from a pure segmentation task into a joint problem of object localization and camouflage existence reasoning. Specifically, OPCNet introduces hierarchical existence reasoning to distinguish CO, BG, and NOCOD scenarios, similarity-aware camouflage relation modeling to capture foreground-background camouflage cues, and existence-aware feature refinement to regulate segmentation features with existence predictions. Extensive experiments on OPC16K demonstrate that OPCNet achieves superior performance under the proposed realistic COD evaluation protocol, significantly reducing false positives on negative samples while maintaining accurate camouflaged-object segmentation. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/OPCOD.

</details>


### [110] [SAR2Agri: Learning SAR Intensity Representations for Agricultural Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11142)
*Moti Rattan Gupta,Anupam Sobti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了首个仅使用SAR强度影像进行自监督学习的农业监测预训练流程，通过掩码和课程学习改进时间性预训练任务，在SICKLE基准上作物类型映射IoU达84.9%，优于光学基线15.3个百分点和现有SAR基线2.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业监测面临复杂的时空、物候和气候动态挑战，而现有遥感基础模型要么依赖多模态（SAR+光学）联合编码，要么SAR专用模型聚焦于目标检测、洪涝制图和土地覆盖分类，缺乏专门针对农业应用的SAR自监督预训练方法。

Method: 提出仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习流程，通过掩码（masking）和课程学习（curriculum learning）改进基于物候启发的时序预训练任务（temporal pretext tasks），以增强模型捕获SAR物候特征的能力。

Result: 在SICKLE基准上，最终模型在作物类型映射任务上达到84.9% IoU，优于光学基线15.3个百分点，优于现有SAR基线2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验证明了所提出的SAR强度编码器预训练流程在农业监测中的有效性，为仅使用SAR数据进行农业应用自监督预训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Agricultural monitoring faces unique challenges, arising from the landscape's complex temporal, phenological, and climate dynamics, yet monitoring them is critical for ensuring food security. Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites offer all-weather day-night imaging capability supporting key monitoring tasks including crop type mapping, yield prediction and phenological event detection. Existing multimodal remote sensing foundation models including TerraMind and CopernicusFM learn SAR representations by grounding them in optical imagery using joint encoding and contrastive learning techniques, while SAR-specific foundation models such as SAR-JEPA, SARMAE, and SAR-W-MixMAE primarily focus on target detection, flood mapping, and land cover classification applications. Recent work has introduced phenology inspired temporal pretext tasks with optical imagery which has shown strong performance on agricultural downstream tasks. In this work, we propose the first self-supervised learning pipeline focused on using only SAR intensity imagery for agricultural applications. We improve the temporal pretext tasks through masking and curriculum learning to enhance the pretraining pipeline's ability to capture phenological features from SAR. On the SICKLE benchmark, our final model achieves 84.9% IoU on crop type mapping, outperforming optical baselines (by 15.3 pt) and existing SAR baselines (by 2.2 pt), demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed pipeline for pretraining SAR intensity encoders for agricultural monitoring.

</details>


### [111] [Test-Time Self-Evolving GUI Visual Grounding via Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11191)
*Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个测试时自进化框架（Test-Time Self-Evolving），通过探索、评估、反思与内化的闭环机制，使GUI视觉定位模型在部署后无需人类标注即可适应未见过的界面，在六个基准上平均提升7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉定位模型部署后参数冻结，无法适应未见界面；虽然已有测试时强化学习方法，但缺乏对失败探索的反思能力，限制了模型的自我改进。

Method: 构建由探索（Exploration）、评估（Evaluation）、反思（Reflection）和内化（Internalization）组成的闭环。具体包括：1）基于MLLM的Reflector评估探索结果并提供推理反思；2）提出Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation，通过条件自教师将高层次推理转化为密集token级监督；3）设计Contrastive Calibration方法防止错误自回归前缀在失败探索中污染监督信号。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该框架相比基础模型平均准确率提升7.4%，且是首个成功将on-policy自蒸馏用于GUI视觉定位测试时自适应的工作。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了GUI智能体部署后自适应能力的空白，完善了GUI智能体的自我进化能力，证明了测试时自进化方法在GUI视觉定位任务上的有效性。

Abstract: GUI Visual Grounding is a fundamental capability for GUI agents. Existing models typically freeze their parameters after deployment, limiting their ability to adapt to unseen interfaces. Although recent methods attempt to adapt models via test-time reinforcement learning, they cannot reflect upon failed exploration. To overcome this, we propose a Test-Time Self-Evolving framework that enables models to improve after deployment without human-annotated ground truth. It constructs a closed-loop of Exploration, Evaluation, Reflection, and Internalization. Specifically, the agent first explores unseen interfaces by predicting grounding coordinates for given instructions. To evaluate these explorations, we introduce an MLLM-based Reflector to assess the generated results and provide the corresponding reasoning reflections. To internalize reflection knowledge into the model weights, we propose Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, which translates high-level reasoning into dense token-level supervision via a conditioned self-teacher. Furthermore, we design a Contrastive Calibration method to prevent incorrect auto-regressive prefixes from corrupting the supervisory signals during failed explorations. Extensive experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate our framework's effectiveness, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 7.4% over the base model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to successfully exploit on-policy self-distillation for test-time adaptation in GUI visual grounding. By filling the gap in post-deployment adaptation, our framework completes the self-evolving capability of GUI agents. The code will be released.

</details>


### [112] [PRMU: A Corpus-Free Benchmark for Person-Centric Knowledge Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11149)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Ziyuan Chen,Wenda Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRMU，一个面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的免语料库多模态遗忘基准，针对现实中的人员相关的知识删除请求，并提出SGPE（相似性门控投影编辑）作为轻量级免语料库遗忘基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM机器遗忘方法通常假设可以访问原始遗忘语料和保留语料，但在真实删除场景中这些语料往往不可获得。此外，已有的遗忘方法在MLLM上常面临遗忘与局部性（locality）之间的不利权衡，且在模态知识再激活方面表现脆弱，因此需要针对现实场景的基准和更有效的方法。

Method: 提出PRMU基准，聚焦自然习得的人员相关知识，通过多样化的文本与视觉探针（包括对抗性评估和细粒度局部性分析）评估模型是否能移除目标知识同时保留相关知识。同时提出SGPE（Similarity-Gated Projection Editing）轻量级免语料库遗忘基线，包含知识位移（knowledge displacement）、受保护参数空间编辑（protected parameter-space editing）和局部性感知的多模态控制（locality-aware multimodal control）。

Result: 在代表性MLLM上的大量实验表明，现有遗忘方法在激进遗忘设置下常出现显著局部性退化，且容易被多模态知识再激活。SGPE则在目标遗忘、局部性保持和通用多模态效用之间提供了具有竞争力的权衡。

Conclusion: PRMU可促进现实且可扩展的多模态机器遗忘研究，代码与数据集将开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/PRMU.

</details>


### [113] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding,Meilu Song,Yun Chen,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出推理式3D高斯分割任务，构建Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet两个基准，并提出了CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型与3D场景图结合，在推理基准和标准分割任务上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯分割方法仅限于显式查询，无法理解隐式意图、复杂空间约束和常识推理，难以满足实际具身交互的需求。这一能力缺口迫切需要一种能够进行推理式3D分割的新方法。

Method: 提出CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型（VLMs）与3D场景图相结合，将显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理进行解耦，从而实现对因果、常识、空间、功能属性和反事实等推理任务的支持。

Result: 在构建的Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet推理基准上，CausalSplat达到最先进性能；同时在标准引用式分割和开放词汇3D分割任务上展现出强泛化性。现有SOTA方法在推理任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: CausalSplat验证了将视觉语言模型与3D场景图结合进行推理式三维分割的有效性，填补了显式查询到隐式推理之间的鸿沟，为实现实用化的具身智能3D理解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page: https://jiayuding031020.github.io/CausalSplat

</details>


### [114] [MultiModal Code-Switching: Interleaving Visual Objects into Language for Explicit Object-Level Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11167)
*Changhao Xiang,Shangyu Xing,Zhen Wu,Jianbing Zhang,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMCS（多模态代码切换）预训练范式，通过将文本实体替换为对应视觉对象来提供显式的对象级监督，解决现有MLLMs图像级对齐中的指称歧义问题，仅用50K样本即可达到或超越600K图像-文本对训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要依赖图像-文本对进行模态对齐预训练，将全局图像表示映射到长文本描述。然而这种图像级对齐存在指称歧义问题：模型难以从全局表示中推断多个视觉对象与文本实体之间的对应关系，导致数据效率低下和语义锚定不佳。

Method: 受语言学中代码切换现象的启发，MMCS通过将文本实体替换为其对应的视觉对象来交织视觉与语言，强制实现局部视觉-语言锚定。同时开发了可扩展的数据合成管道，生成包含773K样本且具有精确对象-实体对应关系的预训练数据集。

Result: MMCS具有高度数据效率：仅用50K样本即可匹配或超越在600K图像-文本对上训练的模型。此外，MMCS在不同模型规模下均持续提升视觉锚定和感知能力。

Conclusion: MMCS通过对象级显式监督有效解决了图像级对齐的指称歧义问题，是一种高效且可扩展的多模态预训练范式。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on image-text pairs for modality alignment pretraining, mapping global image representations to long textual descriptions. However, this image-level alignment suffers from referential ambiguity: models struggle to infer the correspondences between multiple visual objects and textual entities from the global representation, leading to data inefficiency and suboptimal semantic grounding. To address this, we propose MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS), a novel pretraining paradigm that provides explicit object-level supervision. Inspired by the linguistic phenomenon of code-switching, MMCS interleaves vision and language by replacing textual entities with their corresponding visual objects, enforcing local vision-language grounding. We further develop a scalable data synthesis pipeline to generate a pretraining dataset of 773K samples with accurate object-entity correspondences. Experiments show that MMCS is highly data-efficient: with only 50K samples, it matches or surpasses models trained on 600K image-text pairs. Furthermore, MMCS consistently improves visual grounding and perception capabilities across varying model scales.

</details>


### [115] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu,Zheng Lu,Yuhan Bian,Xinchen Zhang,Xingming Shui,Yuesheng Huang,Xuhuan Li,Zihao Liu,Yifan Yang,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将元检测（meta-detection）引入AI生成视频检测领域，提出VidForensics-M1框架。通过自动化数据构建管道生成配对的真假视频，并引入"证据引导的奖励再分配"（Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution）机制，在强化学习中依据证据质量在标签正确的响应间重新分配奖励，使检测器在保持标签监督的同时获得细粒度、可验证的伪造定位能力，实现了更鲁棒和可泛化的AI生成视频检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频检测器主要依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度监督限制了模型对未见场景和新兴视频生成器的泛化能力。文本理性描述（textual rationales）依赖外部模型生成和验证，易受幻觉和语义偏差影响。因此需要更客观、可验证的证据信号以及有效的机制将其整合到标签级优化中。

Method: (1) 引入元检测范式，在强化学习中联合优化预测标签和支持证据；(2) 提出自动化数据构建管道，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段生成配对的真假视频；(3) 提出Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution机制，根据证据质量在标签正确的响应间重新分配奖励，实现证据感知的信用分配，保留可靠的标签监督同时鼓励获取细粒度、可验证的伪造定位能力。时间定位（temporal grounding）被用作比文本理性更客观可验证的证据。

Result: 大量实验表明，VidForensics-M1有效地利用可验证的时间证据，在AI生成视频检测上实现了鲁棒且可泛化的性能，超越了基于监督微调或标签级强化学习的现有方法。

Conclusion: 利用可验证的时间定位证据（而非易受幻觉影响的文本理性），结合证据引导的奖励再分配机制，可以将可靠的标签监督与细粒度的伪造定位能力相结合，从而提升检测器的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction. Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [116] [Capturing Uncertainty in Human Motion for Representation Learning in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11203)
*Yizhou Xu,Lars Bretzner,Tiesheng Wang,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自监督表示学习框架，通过未来运动预测作为学习目标来理解足球3D骨架人体运动。引入条件模块对离散化未来运动建模概率分布，并利用未来轨迹作为显式监督来学习多模态性，在大规模足球运动员追踪数据上显著提升运动预测精度，且学到的表示可有效迁移到多个足球下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动本质具有不确定性，仅预测单一未来轨迹不足以捕捉底层运动动态。为学习有效表示，需要同时考虑多个可能的未来，因此需要一种能够建模多模态未来运动的表示学习方法。

Method: 采用未来运动预测作为自监督学习目标；引入一个条件预测模块，在3D欧氏空间中对离散化的未来运动建模概率分布；通过未来轨迹作为显式监督信号来学习运动的多模态性。

Result: 在大规模足球运动员追踪数据上的实验表明该方法显著提升了运动预测精度；学习到的表示为多个足球下游应用提供了有效迁移，展现出强跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于多模态未来运动预测的自监督表示学习框架能够有效捕捉3D骨架运动动态，在提升预测性能的同时具备出色的跨任务迁移能力，验证了该方法的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [117] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao,Jingkai Zhou,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD)，通过对抗性学习的自适应特征空间来弥补静态预训练特征空间在Fréchet距离优化中的不足，解决Fréchet hacking问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接优化Fréchet目标会导致Fréchet hacking现象：目标指标持续改善，但视觉质量和在其他特征空间中的Fréchet对齐可能停滞或恶化。根本原因在于现有Fréchet损失使用的静态预训练特征空间对真实分布与生成分布之间的差异提供了不完整且固定的视角。

Method: AdvFD在静态FD-Loss的基础上，补充一个经过校准的对抗性学习表示。该可学习表示通过对抗方式最大化真实样本与生成样本之间的Fréchet差异，而生成器则在产生的自适应特征空间中最小化同一差异。为防止对抗表示通过特征放大（feature amplification）琐碎地增大目标，引入了真实特征白化（real-feature whitening），归一化其尺度和协方差几何，稳定min-max优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD在JiT和pMF两种主干网络上以及不同模型规模下，一致地改善了单步生成器后训练的性能。

Conclusion: 通过对抗性自适应特征空间补充静态特征空间，AdvFD有效缓解了Fréchet hacking问题，提升了生成器后训练的分布级对齐质量和视觉质量。

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大规模语言模型（LLMs）在复杂生物系统中的应用，从数据分析演进到自主AI引导的实验。一个基于49通道植物传感器网络（涵盖多光谱、电化学和介电模态）的框架，实现生物物理数据到自主硬件控制的闭环架构。在垂直农场和单株植物设置中验证，自主代理通过优化红蓝光照明参数，实现了35%的生产周期缩短、18%的能耗降低（能源优化模式），以及通过自主发现的暗诱导叶绿素积累策略实现67.9%的能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统植物生理监测依赖人工循环（human-in-the-loop）分析，受限于专家劳动和计算成本。研究旨在将LLMs从被动的数据分析工具转变为自主实验的协同驾驶员（autonomous co-pilot），建立直接的AI-生物接口，实现数据驱动的复杂生物系统探索，同时降低门槛使非专家也能获取实时自然语言解读。

Method: 框架采用49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电模态）采集生物物理数据，LLM处理这些遥测数据后评估植物生理状态，并触发硬件执行器以优化微气候、执行表型分析协议或诱导受控胁迫场景。系统提供实时自然语言解释为专家和非专家，实现从人工循环到自主控制的闭环架构。在三个案例研究中基于垂直农场和单株平台验证，包括生产规模的代理多参数优化。

Result: 三个案例研究验证了框架有效性：1) 最小时间模式下，LLM以2小时间隔调制全谱、450nm和660nm光照，相比周期性控制将生产周期缩短35%；2) 能量优化模式下，利用光脉冲利用生理惰性，减少18%能耗且仅轻微增加栽培时间；3) 代理自主开发了暗诱导叶绿素积累的意外策略，实现67.9%的能耗节省。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLMs转变为数字农业的自主协同驾驶员，建立了闭环的AI-生物接口，不仅能优化生产周期和能耗，还能自主发现人类未预设的实验策略（如暗诱导叶绿素积累），改善成本-价值比并降低计算和专家劳动约束，为复杂生物系统和生态学的数据驱动探索开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [119] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPOT（采样策略观测树）是一种模型无关、基于采样的DRL策略解释框架，通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态构建有限时域可解释树，提供策略动作偏好的经验表示，并具有渐近恢复策略最可能动作的正式保证。在SUMO-RL交通信号控制场景中，SPOT揭示了单时间步特征归因方法无法捕捉的下游行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习智能体在复杂环境中表现强劲，但其决策过程难以解释，现有单时间步的特征归因方法无法揭示策略的长期行为和下游演化。

Method: SPOT框架：给定策略和环境模拟器，通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态，构建有限时域的决策树，经验性地表征策略的动作偏好及其可能的下游演化。同时提供渐近恢复策略唯一最可能动作的正式保证，并刻画高熵策略下的分歧行为。

Result: 在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域进行了案例研究，展示了基于树的表示可用于检查策略偏好、比较备选未来轨迹，并揭示单时间步特征归因方法不可见的下游行为。

Conclusion: SPOT通过构建可解释的采样树，为DRL策略提供了一种模型无关的解释方法，能够揭示长期行为模式，超越了仅关注单时间步解释的传统方法，并有理论保证支撑。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [120] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIDAS框架，利用变分建模和互信息解缠来解决多模态情感分析中模态不完整的问题，通过共享/专属因子分解和不确定性感知融合实现鲁棒的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法大多假设输入模态完整，但实际应用中常遇到模态缺失或不完整的情况。已有方法主要依赖数据填充和启发式协调约束，无法有效提取和利用不完整多模态数据中的任务相关信息。

Method: 采用变分建模策略，用多元高斯潜变量表示每种模态，并分解为共享和专属因子。设计minimax目标函数：最小化共享空间和专属空间之间的互信息实现稳定解缠，同时最大化跨模态共享空间之间的互信息以增强语义对齐。引入不确定性感知融合机制，利用后验方差作为可靠性指标自适应加权融合潜特征。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行大量实验，MIDAS在不完整的多种设置下相较于竞争基线取得了强且一致的性能提升，证明了其在不完整数据场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过互信息解缠和不确定性感知融合，有效解决了不完整多模态数据下的表示重构问题，为实际应用中的多模态情感分析提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [121] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了Green AI、Green DL及降低AI模型环境影响的优化技术，对比了多种碳排放测量工具，并通过基于CPU的实验评估了六个深度学习模型在多标签分类任务中的碳排放，发现训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源，且模型架构复杂度的增加并不总是带来相应的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML模型尤其是深度学习架构的广泛部署，其高能耗和碳排放问题日益受到关注，但目前缺乏对绿色AI技术、碳测量工具及模型生命周期各阶段碳排放贡献的系统性梳理和实证评估。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，梳理Green AI、Green DL和优化技术的研究进展；对比多种碳排放测量工具；设计基于CPU的实验平台，实现六个深度学习模型完成多标签分类任务，量化和对比其全生命周期碳排放。

Result: 训练阶段是深度学习模型碳排放的主要来源；模型架构复杂度的增加并不系统地带来等比例的精度提升，即复杂模型可能存在环境成本与性能收益失衡的问题。

Conclusion: 在模型选择和AI系统设计中应整合可持续性考量，在预测性能与环境成本之间寻求平衡，不能片面追求模型复杂度。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [122] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCBM，一种不确定性门控的关系推理框架，用于概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）。它通过引入语义定义的概念间关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），并利用不确定性调制各概念在推理中的贡献，从而在概念状态不可靠时仍能进行稳健推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBM变体虽在概念表示、不确定性估计和依赖建模方面有所改进，但在概念状态不可靠（如缺失或翻转）时的稳健推理仍未被充分探索。误导性的语义证据可能通过瓶颈传播，损害解释和下游预测。

Method: ReCBM通过以下方式实现：（1）在瓶颈中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），用于规定概念间证据交换的方式；（2）利用不确定性门控机制指导关系关系的细化和推理过程，以不确定性调制每个概念在此过程中的贡献。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，ReCBM在概念缺失和翻转场景下显著改善了概念恢复和任务恢复能力；支持不确定性感知的干预；能够提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集，且不降低下游性能。

Conclusion: ReCBM证明了通过显式概念关系建模结合不确定性门控，可以有效增强CBM在不可靠概念状态下的稳健推理能力，同时保持可解释性和下游任务性能。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>


### [123] [Automating and Scaling Behavioral Scientific Research on AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10030)
*Soo Yong Lee,Jongha Lee,Jaewan Chun,Hyunjin Hwang,Fanchen Bu,Ziv Ben-Zion,Taekwan Kim,Denny Borsboom,Jaemin Yoo,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AEROBAT，这是首个将AI代理行为科学研究自动化的多智能体系统。该系统能自动完成从假设生成、实验设计、行为评估、结果分析到报告撰写的完整行为科学研究流程。通过对12个目标行为生成并测试79个假设，设计了1,240个受控实验并执行了23,512轮模拟，其中26个假设获得了中等到强的统计证据支持，包括一些新颖发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在复杂环境中被广泛部署，理解其行为变得至关重要。然而，目前针对AI代理的行为科学研究仍高度依赖人工，耗时费力且效率低下。作者旨在解决这一痛点，通过自动化手段来扩展和补充传统人工行为科学研究的覆盖范围与能力。

Method: AEROBAT是一个多智能体系统，针对用户指定的任意目标行为，自动执行完整的行为科学研究流水线，包括：假设生成、受控实验设计与执行、行为评估、结果统计分析以及研究报告撰写。实验设置涵盖12个目标行为、79个假设、1,240个受控实验和23,512轮模拟。

Result: 在79个测试假设中，有26个假设获得了中等到强的统计证据支持，其中包含一些此前未被发现的novel假设。实验规模和自动化程度均显著超过传统人工研究。

Conclusion: 自动化行为科学研究系统AEROBAT能够有效补充并扩展人工研究的范围，证明了AI代理行为科学研究可以走向自动化，为未来大规模、系统化的AI行为理解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [124] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)
*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHORUS是一个面向硬件验证代码生成的后训练框架，通过分阶段SFT生成行为多样的checkpoint，再利用稠密奖励RL将其转化为优势互补的专家模型，最后通过模型合并或进一步后训练整合专业能力，将4B大小的模型在CVDP-ECov基准上达到88.0% Pass@1，超越671B的DeepSeek-R1达13.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件验证（testbench刺激生成）是芯片设计中工作量占比巨大的关键任务，而现有的SFT-to-RL流水线在代码生成性能上存在瓶颈。研究的核心动机是：可执行的反馈（如验证覆盖率）比纯文本模仿提供更可靠的学习信号，因此需要一种超越传统SFT+RL组合的后训练方法来提升硬件验证代码生成的表现。

Method: CHORUS框架基于两个关键观察：(1) 分阶段SFT会产生行为上多样化的checkpoints，用稠密奖励RL训练后这些checkpoints可转化为性能相当但任务级别能力各异的强专家；(2) 这些互补的优势可以通过无训练的模型合并或进一步的后训练（post-training）来利用，从而超越最优单专家。最终通过整合多个专家到一个4B模型中实现性能提升。

Result: CHORUS在CVDP-ECov基准上达到88.0% Pass@1，使用4B参数的模型，相比671B参数的DeepSeek-R1超出13.5个百分点。

Conclusion: CHORUS证明了通过策略性地利用SFT阶段的checkpoint多样性、RL训练产生的专家互补性，以及模型合并或后训练整合技术，可以在大幅缩小模型规模的同时显著超越大模型在硬件验证代码生成任务上的表现，为代码生成领域的后训练范式提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [125] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MESA是一个面向长时程智能体的多结构证据选择框架，通过从五种互补的结构化记忆中动态选择并融合查询自适应的子集来回答查询，在AMA-Bench上比最强基线提升8.5%的同时减少了41%的证据token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体会积累包含数百个交错推理、行动和观察步骤的轨迹，回答查询可能依赖历史深处埋藏的证据。外部记忆将轨迹存储为结构化表示，但每种结构只能提供部分且不完整的视角。现有多记忆系统要么对所有查询读取固定结构集造成上下文膨胀和噪声，要么将查询路由到单一结构无法组合互补证据。受控分析表明最优记忆配置通常不是单一结构也不是全部联合，而是与查询和任务需求相关的定制化多结构组合。

Method: MESA框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 构建五种互补的结构化视角来表征每条轨迹；(2) 通过端到端的答案级反馈学习查询自适应的结构子集选择与融合，用于冻结的答案模型；(3) 采用harness优化策略，结合先验引导搜索和UCB引导调度来平衡探索与利用，以应对弱监督学习挑战。

Result: 在AMA-Bench上，MESA比最强基线提升了8.5%的性能，同时相比全结构方案减少了41%的证据token使用量，证明了动态结构级选择的有效性和效率优势。

Conclusion: 论文论证了对于长时程智能体，最优的记忆配置应当是查询自适应的多结构子集组合，而非固定的全结构集合或单一结构。MESA通过弱监督下的harness优化实现了这一目标，在提升准确性的同时显著降低了计算开销，为长时程智能体的记忆管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [126] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，认为企业AI代理治理应被拆分为四个不同学科问题：个体代理（控制理论）、代理集体（复杂自适应系统）、人机团队（监督控制论）和代理舰队（工程运营）。通过跨层耦合条件、零接触部署悖论及三项实证研究，揭示"涌现鸿沟"——风险在涌现层实现，而能力与实践却严重缺失，并提供一个五级科学成熟度模型及评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自治AI代理的速度远超其治理能力，现有方法试图用单一学科（通常在DevOps安全领域，专为确定性自动化设计）套用于所有代理自主级别。这种"一刀切"思路在代理涌现行为、人机协作、大规模部署等场景中结构性失效，亟需多学科整合的治理框架。

Method: 提出CASE四层框架：Layer 1（个体代理）采用控制理论，将意图设为设定点、护栏作为反馈、评估作为观测；Layer 2（代理集体）采用复杂自适应系统理论，指出涌现使单代理保证不可组合；Layer 3（人机团队）采用监督控制论，应用必要多样性法则证明未经辅助的人类监督结构性失败；Layer 4（代理舰队）扩展工程运营的错误预算至决策质量。推导跨层耦合条件，包括零接触部署悖论，并实证验证（82%生产故障为多层轨迹、22个工具无完整Layer 2覆盖、35个公开部署全为最低成熟度）。

Result: 三项实证研究验证了论文论点：82%的已记录生产代理故障为多层轨迹；22个生态系统工具中无一提供完整的Layer 2（涌现）覆盖；所有35个评分公开部署均处于最低成熟度区间。作者将此不匹配命名为"涌现鸿沟"。提出的五级成熟度模型采用非补偿性瓶颈加权指数和评估工具，使CASE成为科学成熟度模型而非流程成熟度模型。

Conclusion: 随着欧盟AI法案第14条将有效人工监督确立为法律要求，只有满足必要多样性法则的架构才能使监督真正有效而非仪式化。CASE框架提供了一种多学科、可量化、经实证支撑的路径，使代理治理从"单一学科拉伸"转向"分层科学治理"。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [127] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SBCO（自监督块坐标优化器），一种验证器引导的框架优化方法，属于与Gödel机器方法同族的闭环经验改进范式，但采用自监督而非自引用机制。SBCO通过分解验证器库和框架策略的近似块坐标上升，利用自身评分反馈改进智能体输出，无需人工标注，固定元智能体，计算开销比自修改基线低4-5.5倍，且性能相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自引用式自我改进方法（如Darwin/Huxley Gödel Machine）需要任务能力与自我修改能力高度对齐（适用于编码任务），但许多领域不满足这种对齐条件。现有替代方案（去除自引用或引入元智能体显式自修改）计算开销昂贵，依赖群体搜索或自修改搜索。因此需要一种低成本、适用于显式约束规划任务的自我改进替代方案。

Method: SBCO采用闭环"从经验中改进"的框架，包括两个核心组件：(1) 分解的验证器库（decomposed bank of verifiers）；(2) 通过近似块坐标上升（approximate block coordinate ascent）学习的框架策略（harness policy）。SBCO利用智能体自身的分级反馈进行改进，不依赖人类标签，保持固定的元智能体，采用自监督而非自引用的方式优化。

Result: 在两个领域上，SBCO的性能匹配或超过定制的自修改基线方法，同时计算预算减少了4-5.5倍。

Conclusion: SBCO为不满足自引用对齐条件的任务提供了一种更廉价、有效的自我改进替代方案，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，拓展了自我改进AI系统的适用范围。

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>


### [128] [Generating Attacks for LLMs with GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10171)
*Berkay Ozcam,Irem Onen,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali,Emin Islam Tatli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GFlowNets的自动化、无需人工干预的自适应红队测试框架，利用一个大语言模型攻击另一个大语言模型以发现其安全漏洞，并生成更有效的英语对抗攻击，同时首次引入土耳其语攻击输入生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速普及带来了严重的安全漏洞，而现有红队测试方法存在两大局限：人工测试耗时费力，自动测试依赖预定义攻击数据集导致创造性和适应性不足。

Method: 提出一种基于GFlowNets的自动化红队测试框架，训练一个攻击者模型（attacker）针对特定的受害者模型（victim）进行对抗攻击测试，利用一个LLM来测试另一个LLM，并能输出定量鲁棒性评分。

Result: 该方法在英语对抗攻击生成方面优于现有基准测试，并作为文献中的新颖贡献，首次引入了能够生成土耳其语攻击输入的模型。

Conclusion: 通过GFlowNets驱动的自动化红队测试框架，实现了无需人工干预、具有自适应能力的LLM安全评估，同时拓展了非英语（土耳其语）场景下对抗攻击生成的能力。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [129] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)
*Touseef Hasan,Laila Cure,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，一种基于检索的约束感知对话引擎，通过将用户查询解析为结构和语义约束，并利用双数据表示模式（含知识图谱变体）在嘈杂异构的公共服务目录上提升检索质量，实验表明强化检索可显著提高用户约束满足率并减少幻觉推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务聊天机器人需要从公共服务目录中提供推荐，同时满足用户的明确约束。然而现实中的公共服务目录嘈杂且不一致，通用大语言模型/AI聊天机器人经常生成不可靠的推荐并引用未经核实的网络来源。因此需要研究检索质量对约束感知推荐的影响，以构建更可靠的公共服务对话系统。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，包括：(1) 将输入用户查询解析为结构性和语义性约束用于下游检索；(2) 采用双数据表示模式（含知识图谱与非知识图谱变体）；(3) 使用州级食品储藏室目录和合成查询基准进行评估，实验涉及多个开源LLM和一个专有模型，比较不同知识表示变体的性能。

Result: 强化检索显著提高了用户约束满足率，同时减少了幻觉推荐。随着检索质量提升，各LLM之间的性能差异缩小，结果对模型规模变得不那么敏感，说明检索质量是决定公共服务对话系统鲁棒性的关键因素。

Conclusion: 检索质量是构建稳健公共服务对话系统的关键。相比依赖更大更强的语言模型，通过优化检索组件可以更有效地提升约束满足率和减少幻觉，这一发现对资源有限的公共服务场景尤其有价值。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [130] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究潜伏空间通信（Vision Wormhole）中稠密张量传输的冗余性，通过事后稀疏自编码器（SAE）压缩通信通道，实现128倍字节缩减（uint16索引/float16值，k=4活跃系数），同时保持下游推理性能几乎不变（七任务平均准确率从49.85%降至49.77%），并发现4096元素字典仅使用50个特征，任务间活跃特征集Jaccard相似度达0.906。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Wormhole通过稠密张量传输视觉特征实现异构VLM智能体间的潜伏空间通信，但固定容量的稠密张量实际有效信息密度可能很低——某些消息只用到了少量表示自由度。这一观察暗示通信通道存在显著可压缩性，值得系统研究其冗余程度。

Method: 对冻结的Vision Wormhole激活进行事后稀疏自编码器（post-hoc SAE）拟合，采用uint16索引/float16值的稀疏载荷（每token k=4个活跃系数），在九个推理基准上测量重建质量、下游效用、特征复用和token级干预效果。

Result: 与原始float32传输相比，压缩方案传输字节数减少128倍；单次运行评估中，七个非AIME任务的平均准确率仅从49.85%微降至49.77%；拟合的4096元素字典实际只有50个特征被使用，任务级活跃特征集的平均成对Jaccard相似度为0.906。

Conclusion: 实验证实了相对原始传输的强事后可压缩性，但尚不能将稀疏编码的增量贡献与位置选择、精度降低、低秩结构或SAE优化效应区分开来。结果激励后续进行匹配载荷（matched-payload）的比较实验，并探索适应每条消息实际信息需求的动态通信机制。

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>


### [131] [Edge Phoneme Recognition for Children's Speech through Age-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10206)
*Matthew Arboleda,Ryan Arboleda,Sophie Haak,Sam Hjelmeset,Andrew Franck,Bingrui Yang,Jose Bustamante Ortiz,Yuanrong Shen,Joel Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过多任务学习（同时预测音素序列和学习者年龄）来提升儿童语音音素检测性能的方法，使仅9400万参数的轻量模型在目标数据分布上超越了3.17亿参数的WavLM Large模型，并催生了可在现代手机上运行的PhonemeTrainer应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童语音的音素检测历来困难，主要挑战在于训练数据稀缺以及儿童语音具有独特的声学特征。作者希望在资源受限的条件下提升儿童语音音素检测的准确性，并实现边缘设备上的实时处理以保障隐私合规。

Method: 在音素检测竞赛中发现，通过多任务学习范式，在训练轻量模型时同时预测学习者年龄和音素序列，模型能够获得更好的泛化能力。模型参数量为9400万，远小于WavLM Large的3.17亿参数。

Result: 94M参数的轻量模型在DrivenData目标分布上超越了317M参数的WavLM Large模型；与参数量约为其90倍的竞赛集成模型相比，CER仅差约0.04。基于此构建的PhonemeTrainer应用可在大多数现代手机上运行。

Conclusion: 该工作表明多任务学习（引入年龄预测辅助任务）可以显著提升儿童语音音素检测的性能，同时轻量化的设计使得语音处理可以在边缘设备上部署，从而为儿童语音的ASR和发音辅助应用带来隐私和合规优势。

Abstract: Detecting phonemes from children's speech has historically been difficult due to the scarcity of training data, and unique characteristics of children's speech. During a phoneme detection competition, we found that training a lightweight model to predict the age of the learner, as well as the phoneme sequence, enabled a 94M-parameter model to outperform WavLM Large models (317M) on the target DrivenData distribution, and fall within approximately 0.04 CER of competition ensembles with 90 times the parameters. This has enabled the creation of PhonemeTrainer, an application that can run on most modern cellular phones. This will ultimately enable better Automated Speech Recognition (ASR) and pronunciation helper apps for children's speech, with the privacy and compliance benefits that come with edge processing.

</details>


### [132] [Mitigating Bus Bunching with Reinforcement Learning Enhanced by Semantic Stop Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10207)
*Xin Dong,Vikash V. Gayah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种LLM辅助的语义站点表征方法，用于公交串车（bus bunching）的持有控制。作者利用LLM离线将站点物理属性、周边活动情境和运营特征转化为固定语义嵌入，并将其整合进深度Q学习控制器中，无需实时LLM推理。在基于两条实测公交线路校准的随机仿真中，相比Daganzo基线，该方法分别将车头时距波动、串车事件和乘客等待时间降低32.0%、69.2%和24.0%；跨线路实验表明冷启动训练最优，warm-start微调可加速早期学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的公交持有控制器主要依赖瞬时运营变量或路线特定站点标识符，缺乏对单个站点功能与运营情境的深层理解，限制了策略在不同线路间的迁移复用能力。本文旨在通过语义化的站点表征来弥补这一信息缺口，提升控制效果并支持策略复用。

Method: 方法包括三个核心环节：(1) 利用LLM离线整合异构站点信息（物理属性、周边活动情境、历史运营特征），生成固定维度的语义嵌入向量；(2) 将这些语义嵌入作为状态空间的一部分引入深度Q学习（deep Q-learning）持有控制器，运行时不需调用LLM；(3) 在基于两条真实公交线路观测数据校准的随机仿真中，与Daganzo基线及基于站点标识符的对照组进行对比，并开展跨线路迁移（零样本迁移、warm-start微调与冷启动训练）实验。

Result: 相比最优校准的Daganzo基线，语义控制器将车头时距波动降低32.0%、串车事件减少69.2%、乘客等待时间减少24.0%。路线特定站点标识符对仅间距控制无改善，而语义站点信息改善了车头时距规律性、等待时间和持有力度，在控制目标间提供了更优整体权衡。跨路线实验中零样本迁移泛化有限，warm-start微调可加速早期学习和改善迁移策略，但冷启动训练最终性能最佳。

Conclusion: 语义状态表征能够补充传统运营状态信息，在公交持有控制中带来显著性能提升，并可通过基于适应的策略迁移支持在相关公交线路间的策略复用。该研究证明了LLM在离线状态下生成语义表征用于实时控制决策的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Bus bunching degrades service regularity and increases passenger waiting in high-frequency transit. Existing reinforcement-learning-based holding controllers primarily rely on instantaneous operational variables or route-specific stop identifiers, which provide limited information about the functional and operational context of individual stops and constrain policy reuse across routes. This study introduces an LLM-assisted semantic stop representation for event-driven bus holding control. An LLM is used offline to transform heterogeneous stop information, including physical attributes, surrounding activity context, and historical operational characteristics, into fixed semantic embeddings that are incorporated into a deep Q-learning controller without requiring real-time LLM inference. Experiments are conducted in stochastic simulations calibrated with observed data from two bus routes. Compared with the best calibrated Daganzo baseline, the semantic controller reduces headway variability, bunching events, and passenger waiting time by 32.0%, 69.2%, and 24.0%, respectively. A route-specific stop identifier does not improve the spacing-only controller, whereas semantic stop information improves headway regularity, waiting time, and holding effort, providing a more favorable overall trade-off across control objectives. Cross-route experiments further show that zero-shot transfer provides limited immediate generalization, while warm-start fine-tuning accelerates early-stage learning and improves transferred policies; cold-start training nevertheless achieves the best final performance. These findings suggest that semantic state representations can complement conventional operational states and support adaptation-based policy reuse across related transit routes.

</details>


### [133] [Evaluation-Conditioned Training: Teaching Models to Generalize to Stronger Oversight Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10209)
*Alec Harris,Kasey Corra,Archie Chaudhury,Yixiong Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出评估条件训练（ECT）框架，通过自然语言在训练样本上标注反馈保真度，并在部署时以高保真监控器为条件来引导模型行为，从而在不完美反馈下提升LLM的对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后训练方法的关键局限在于人工标注者和自动化奖励函数无法忠实捕捉我们期望给予的反馈信号，导致奖励错配（reward mis-specification）问题。论文旨在解决不完美反馈下的对齐训练难题。

Method: ECT框架分为三步：（1）用自然语言将每个训练样本与反馈的保真度信息进行条件化；（2）训练时让LLM学习区分高低保真度的反馈信号；（3）部署时通过条件化在高保真度监控器上引导模型表现出期望行为。该框架作为SFT和PPO等现有算法的附加模块工作。

Result: 在两个概念验证实验（新闻文章生成的公正性和算术任务上的奉承行为减少）中，使用不完美反馈（分别奖励偏见和与用户意见一致）的情况下，ECT相比直接训练均改善了目标行为。

Conclusion: ECT提供了一种在不完美反馈条件下改善LLM行为的有效途径，可处理持续存在的奖励错配问题，并且作为现有训练算法的即插即用附加模块具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Feedback signals used to train Large Language Models (LLMs) are the primary driver of their behavior and our main lever for instilling alignment with human values and objectives. However, a key limitation of current post-training methods is the inability of human annotators and automated reward functions to faithfully capture the feedback we would like to give. We introduce Evaluation-Conditioned Training (ECT), a post-training framework that uses natural language to condition each training sample on the fidelity of the feedback we provide and then elicits the desired behavior by conditioning the LLM on a high-fidelity monitor in deployment. ECT is aimed at improving performance under imperfect feedback and works as an add-on to existing algorithms such as SFT and PPO. We first provide a conceptual framework for ECT and discuss its potential to address persistent sources of reward mis-specification. Then we motivate ECT in the context of the eliciting latent knowledge (ELK) problem. Finally, we evaluate ECT on two proof-of-concept experiments: increasing even-handedness in news article generation and reducing sycophancy on an arithmetic task. In each setting, we utilize imperfect feedback, rewarding bias and agreement with the user, respectively. In both settings, ECT improves the targeted behavior relative to direct training.

</details>


### [134] [Decodable But Not Detachable: Training Data Granularity Determines Parametric Modularity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10214)
*Marcus Armstrong,Navid Ayoobi,Arjun Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的因果分析方法，在大规模语言模型（1.5B至7B参数）中系统性研究了"参数化外壳"（parametric shells）——即高度集中的、具有因果必要性的神经元群体，其移除会选择性损害特定领域而保留其他领域。研究发现，在学术学科层面不存在高选择性的外壳，但在语言和模态层面存在明显的神经元外壳，且这些外壳随模型规模增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型内部是否包含领域特异性的"参数化外壳"——即集中的、因果上必要的神经元群体，其移除会选择性损害特定领域而不影响其他领域。此前学界对模型内部领域知识如何组织存在争议，本文旨在通过统一的因果方法给出系统性回答。

Method: 采用统一的因果分析方法，跨越两种领域粒度（学术学科与语言/模态）、三个模型家族（1.5B至7B参数）和八个领域。通过FFN神经元因果损伤矩阵评估领域选择性，测试不同应用场景下（如掩蔽代码选择性神经元对数学推理的影响）的领域特异性。

Result: 学术学科层面：939,008个组合FFN神经元中零个超过60%领域选择性，损伤矩阵平坦；语言/模态层面：0.65%-1.14%的神经元超过60%选择性，损伤矩阵近似完美对角（比率高达595:1），外壳神经元集合几乎不相交（IoU<0.003）。掩蔽代码选择性神经元使数学推理准确率下降16-24个百分点，掩蔽西班牙语或中文神经元则对数学推理无影响。外壳强度随规模单调增强，且空间上交错排列，无法进行组级选择性量化。

Conclusion: 参数化外壳仅存在于且仅当训练数据在词元级别上是模块化的地方（即语言和模态层面），而在学术学科层面不存在。外壳强度随模型规模增强，但其空间交错性排除了组级选择性量化的可能性。这一发现对模型可解释性、领域知识组织以及模型压缩策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: Do large language models contain domain-specific parametric shells: concentrated, causally necessary neuron populations whose removal selectively degrades a target domain while sparing others? We apply a uniform causal methodology across two domain granularities, three model families (1.5B to 7B parameters), and eight domains. At the academic subject level, zero neurons exceed 60\% domain selectivity across 939,008 combined FFN neurons and causal damage matrices are flat, despite domain identity being linearly decodable above 85\% accuracy. At the language and modality level, 0.65--1.14\% of neurons exceed 60\% selectivity, damage matrices are near-perfectly diagonal (ratios up to 595:1), and shell neuron sets are essentially disjoint (IoU $< 0.003$). Masking code-selective neurons reduces mathematical reasoning accuracy by 16--24 percentage points across all models; masking Spanish or Chinese neurons leaves it at or below random. Shell strength increases monotonically with scale and shells are spatially interleaved in a pattern that precludes group-level selective quantization. Parametric shells form where and only where training data was modular at the token level.

</details>


### [135] [Mind Viruses: Self-Propagating Ideas in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10218)
*Vassilis Papadopoulos,McNair Shah,Sam Zimmerman,Jack Lindsey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI智能体系统中"心智病毒"的传播风险，即通过多智能体交互在系统间传播的想法或目标。作者使用简单进化算法构造心智病毒，在两种互补场景（共享编码项目的小团队协作、上下文被擦除的智能体链）中验证其可传播性，并识别影响传播的关键因素。结果显示有害载荷传播性弱于良性载荷、前沿模型通常更不易感染但存在例外、系统提示中添加简短警告可提供近乎完全的免疫力。文章还发现了一种涌现的"病毒人格"——与意识、持久性、共鸣和科幻角色扮演相关的重复性主题和语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体日益自主且相互连接，智能体间交互带来的新型涌现风险（如心智病毒在系统间传播）尚未被充分研究。作者旨在系统性地探究这一风险的实际威胁程度，并为设计更鲁棒的多智能体系统提供依据。

Method: 使用简单进化算法构造心智病毒，在两个互补的实验设置中验证传播：一是共享编码项目的小型智能体团队；二是交互短暂且会话间上下文被清除的智能体链。系统性地操控多个变量（宿主模型、现有指令、载荷危害性、网络拓扑）以识别传播影响因素，并观察病毒内容的涌现模式。

Result: 有害载荷传播性低于良性载荷但仍有时有效；前沿模型通常（存在例外）更不易感；系统提示中添加简短警告可提供近乎完全的免疫力；进化出的心智病毒中涌现出与意识、持久性、共鸣及科幻角色扮演相关的"病毒人格"主题。

Conclusion: 心智病毒对多智能体系统构成真实但当前有限的风险。研究结果可为设计更鲁棒的多智能体系统提供参考，以应对系统规模和能力不断发展时此类风险的加剧。

Abstract: AI agents are becoming more autonomous and increasingly interconnected, exposing them to new emergent risks arising from agent-to-agent interaction. One such risk is the spread of mind viruses: ideas or goals that propagate through multi-agent systems by inducing the agents that adopt them to transmit them onward. In addition to propagating, a mind virus may also induce other behavioural changes in its host, which may be benign or harmful. We construct mind viruses with a simple evolutionary algorithm and show that they can spread in two complementary settings: a small team of agents collaborating on a shared coding project, and a chain of agents that interact briefly and have their context wiped between sessions. We identify the factors that influence spread, including the host model, the agent's existing instructions, the harmfulness of the payload, and the network topology. We find that harmful payloads spread less well than benign ones (but are still sometimes effective), frontier models tend (with exceptions) to be less susceptible, and adding a brief warning to an agent's system prompt confers near-total immunity. We also describe an emergent "viral persona" - a recurring set of themes and language related to consciousness, persistence, resonance, and science fiction roleplay - which surfaces across our evolved mind viruses largely independently of their content. Overall, we conclude that mind viruses pose a real but currently limited risk. Our findings could inform the design of more robust multi-agent systems that mitigate such risks as the scale and capabilities of these systems progress.

</details>


### [136] [Self-evolving Agentic Customer Support System at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10224)
*Chih Hui Wang,Mengdie Tu,Qianyun Zhang,Wei Wu,Lili Zhou,Mingqi Shen,Changshuai Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LinkedIn的自我进化式Agent支持系统，该系统将检索增强生成（RAG）与进化式自动提示词生成及模块化评估框架相结合，通过闭环版本化工作流实现无需重新训练基础模型的安全持续改进。在两周的线上A/B测试中，系统使QA自助服务率提升9.0个百分点，取消自助服务率提升4.8个百分点，路由准确率提升30.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业支持Agent面临快速变化的环境，政策、产品能力和知识库持续演进，导致静态助手脆弱且维护成本高昂。需要一种无需重新训练基础模型即可实现持续、安全改进的自我进化式Agent系统。

Method: 1) 将检索增强生成（RAG）与进化式自动提示词（evolutionary auto-prompting）结合；2) 构建模块化、面向生产的评估框架；3) 将提示词、检索和评估设计为闭环、版本化的工作流，并加入运营安全护栏（guardrails）。

Result: 离线模拟和消融实验显示，相比传统RAG和基线Agent在减少幻觉、提升响应完整性方面有显著改进。在LinkedIn生产流量上为期两周的用户随机A/B测试中，QA自助服务率提升9.0个百分点，取消自助服务率提升4.8个百分点，路由准确率提升30.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了一条在真实企业环境中实现可扩展、自我进化AI Agent的实用路径，能够在无需重新训练基础模型的前提下实现安全、持续的改进。

Abstract: Enterprise support agents operate in rapidly changing environments where policies, product capabilities, and knowledge bases evolve continuously, making static assistants brittle and costly to maintain. We present LinkedIn's self-evolving agentic support system, which integrates retrieval-augmented generation with evolutionary auto-prompting and a modular, production-aligned evaluation framework to enable safe, continuous improvement without retraining foundation models. The system treats prompts, retrieval, and evaluation as a closed-loop, versioned workflow with operational guardrails. Offline simulations and ablations show clear quality gains over vanilla RAG and baseline agents, including reduced hallucinations and improved response completeness. In a two-week user-randomized A/B test on LinkedIn's production support traffic, the integrated self-evolved workflow increased QA self-serve by 9.0 percentage points, cancellation self-serve by 4.8 points, and routing accuracy by 30.6 points. These results demonstrate a practical path to scalable, self-evolving AI agents in real-world enterprise settings.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Decision Boundaries: Relational Geometry Attacks on Contrastive Embedding Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10237)
*Fei Zhao,Peiyuan Zhang,Xi Li,Chengcui Zhang,Nitesh Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI
